         Consulting Services

Enterprise AI Adoption & Enablement




          Addendum No. 1

         RFP 739-SL3821039
                                                        Addendum No. 1

                                             Consulting Services
                                                      –
                                    Enterprise AI Adoption & Enablement
AMENDMENT (1)

         Section 2.4 - Anticipated Term, which previously read:

                  The anticipated term of any resulting agreement will begin on the date an agreement is executed, and expire on
                  August 31, 2027.

         Is deleted in its entirety and replaced with the following:

                  The anticipated term of any resulting agreement will begin on the date an agreement is executed, and expire on
                  March 31, 2028.

AMENDMENT (2)


         Section 5.4 - Services Specifications, paragraph entitled “Project Schedule:”, which previously read:

                  TTUHSC desires to operate in accordance with the following schedule. These dates are for planning purposes
                  and subject to change:

                  •   Kick-off meeting:
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 1: ________________
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 2: ________________
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 3: ________________
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 4: ________________
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 5: ________________
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 6: ________________

         Is deleted in its entirety and replaced with the following:

                  TTUHSC desires to operate in accordance with the following schedule. These dates are for planning purposes
                  and subject to change:

                  •   Kick-off meeting: February 2027;
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 1: April 2027;
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 2: April 2027;
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 3: September 2027;
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 4: September 2027;
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 5: December 2027;
                  •   Completion of Deliverable 6: February 2028.

AMENDMENT (3)

Section 6.1 Pricing for Services Offered:, which previously read:

    1.   Provide a “not to exceed” fixed fee for each of the deliverables including associated fees (i.e. printing costs, attendance
         at meetings, travel), for the Consulting Services - Enterprise AI Adoption & Enablement. A schedule should be included
         to document the level of effort used to derive the quoted fixed fee.

    2.   An hourly resource rate card by resource type (e.g., Project Manager, Analyst) valid through August 31, 2027 which will
         be used as the basis for change orders.
Is deleted in its entirety and replaced with the following:

     1.   Provide a “not to exceed” fixed fee for each of the deliverables including associated fees (i.e. printing costs, attendance
          at meetings, travel), for the Consulting Services - Enterprise AI Adoption & Enablement. A schedule should be included
          to document the level of effort used to derive the quoted fixed fee.

     2.   An hourly resource rate card by resource type (e.g., Project Manager, Analyst) valid through March 31, 2028 which will
          be used as the basis for change orders.

495. Section 5.4 - Technical Requirements: Regarding TX-            Under Texas Government Code Sec. 2054.0593 and Texas Department of
     RAMP certification, is TX-RAMP Level 1 or Level 2               Information Resources guidelines, TX-RAMP applies specifically to Cloud
     required if a vendor-provided orchestration platform            Service Providers offering hosted cloud computing services (SaaS, PaaS, IaaS).
     handles non-PHI operational data?                               Where the solution is delivered as a professional/managed service operating
                                                                     inside TTUHSC's approved enterprise environment, using TTUHSC's certified
                                                                     infrastructure and tooling, with no contractor-hosted cloud service and no
                                                                     institutional data leaving TTUHSC-controlled systems, TX-RAMP would
                                                                     likely not apply. Where a proposal includes a hosted cloud service, TX-RAMP
                                                                     would apply.
                                                                   Where TX-RAMP applies, the level is determined by the data classification
                                                                    handled - generally Level 1 for non-PHI operational data, and Level 2 for
                                                                    confidential/regulated data. Advisory, on-premises, or managed-service
                                                                    delivery inside TTUHSC's environment would generally fall outside TX-
                                                                    RAMP scope.
